CODING AGENT HARNESS · SOURCE AUDIT47f943859bef
DE

DeepSeek Harness
Harness, from source

DeepSeek Harness models an agent as a Cordis plugin tree: models, tools, session logs, permissions, sandboxes, skills, sub-agents, and UI are replaceable services. Its central lesson is the invariant that everything visible to a model must be reconstructable from the session log.

TypeScript / Cordis / Node sandbox runnersPlugin-composed, event-sourced Coding Agent Harnessmain

EXECUTIVE READING

Thesis before source

CONTROL LOOP

Think of DeepSeek Harness as a hot-swappable runtime tree: the event log is the black box, the agent loop is the driver, and model/tools/sandbox are replaceable parts.

EVIDENCE

24 findings / 23 citations

BOUNDARY

The Cordis package graph is large; one request crosses agent-loop, session, tools, llm, and sandbox packages

BUILDING FIT

Plugins, services, and events form replaceable seams; profiles, bundles, and patches can recompose the runtime tree

BORROW

  • Plugins, services, and events form replaceable seams; profiles, bundles, and patches can recompose the runtime tree
  • Turn/step, agent/*, and tools/* events have distinct roles; tools may run in parallel while results commit in model order
  • JSONL logs, checkpoints, compaction, runner probes, and fail-closed sandbox selection make recovery and isolation runtime features

WATCH

  • The Cordis package graph is large; one request crosses agent-loop, session, tools, llm, and sandbox packages
  • sandbox-local depends on bwrap, Landlock, Seatbelt, or Windows ACL capabilities and refuses execution when confinement is unavailable
  • Composition is powerful, but the resolved profile/patch tree must be persisted and audited rather than inferred from the UI

READING MODE

  • Pin the commit before tracing the call chain
  • Separate facts, inferences, and risks
  • Every finding returns to code
00 · METHOD

Method: not a README summary

README POLICY

README locates entrypoints and naming; implementation claims return to runtime code, contracts, or tests.

FACT POLICY

L1 runtime code leads; L2 contracts and L3/L4 evidence cross-check; L5 inferences stay explicit.

  373
  374    ctx.tools.register(defineTool({
  375      name: 'bash',
  376      description: config.description,
  377      parameters: {
  378        command: {
  379          type: 'string',
  380          required: true,
  381          description: 'The bash command to run. Relative path is preferred in the command.',
  382        },
  383      },
  384      output: {
  385        schema: { type: 'string' },
  386        render: (_args, value) => [{ type: 'text', text: value }],
  387      },
  388      async execute(args, exec) {
  389        if (args.command.trim().length === 0) throw new Error('command must be a non-empty string')
  390        const owner = exec.agent
  391        if (owner === undefined) throw new Error('bash requires an owning agent session')
  392        return serialized(owner, async () => {
  393          exec.signal.throwIfAborted()
  394          return executeCommand(ctx, shells, owner, args.command, config, exec.signal)
  395        })
  396      },
  397      presentCall: args => ({ card: 'terminal', title: args.command }),
  398    }))
  399  }
  400
  401  export const name = 'tool-bash-persistent'
  402  export const inject = ['tools', 'terminals']
  403
  404  /** Configuration for the persistent Bash tool. */
  405  export interface Config {
  406    /** PTY backend used for each owner-isolated persistent shell (default `shell`). */
  407    backendType?: string
  408    /** Wall-clock limit for one command (default 300000). */
  409    timeoutMs?: number
  410    /** Maximum returned command-output characters before clipping (default 16000). */
  411    maxOutputChars?: number
  412    /** Model-facing tool description; deployments may describe their environment. */
  413    description?: string
  414  }
  415
  416  /** Runtime configuration schema for the persistent Bash tool. */
  417  export const Config: z<Config> = z.object({
  418    backendType: z.string().default('shell'),
  419    timeoutMs: z.number().default(300_000),
  420    maxOutputChars: z.number().default(16_000),
  421    description: z.string().default(DEFAULT_DESCRIPTION),
  422  })
  423
  424  /** Register one owner-scoped persistent `bash` tool. */
  425  export function apply(ctx: Context, config: Config): void {
  426    const resolved: ResolvedConfig = {
  427      backendType: config.backendType ?? 'shell',
  428      timeoutMs: config.timeoutMs ?? 300_000,
  429      maxOutputChars: config.maxOutputChars ?? 16_000,
  430      description: config.description ?? DEFAULT_DESCRIPTION,
  431    }
  432    if (resolved.backendType.trim().length === 0) {
  433      throw new Error('tool-bash-persistent: backendType must be non-empty')
  434    }

  283            pluginRunId: requireJsonString(result, 'pluginRunId'),
  284          }
  285        },

  290        const packageId = CordisDynamicPackageId(args.packageId)
  291        const receipt = await ctx.dynamicCordisRunner.run(agent, pluginId, packageId, args.mode, exec.signal)

  401  function requireJsonObject(value: JsonValue): Record<string, JsonValue> {
  402    if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) {
  403      throw new Error('expected a JSON object')
  404    }
  405    return value
  406  }

  408  function requireJsonString(value: Record<string, JsonValue>, key: string): string {
  409    const field = value[key]
  410    if (typeof field !== 'string') throw new Error(`expected JSON string field "${key}"`)
  411    return field

  110        }),
  111      },
  112      async execute(args: EditToolArgs, exec) {
  113        const input = parseEditArgs(args)
  114        // Resolve the per-call sandbox policy (approved mode > session override
  115        // > backend default, plus the session cwd root) BEFORE anything executes.
  116        const sandboxPolicy = await sandbox.resolvePolicy('edit', args, exec)
  117        const target = await ctx.fs.resolve(input.filePath, sessionResolveOptions(exec, input.filePath, sandboxPolicy?.workspaceRoot))
  118        // Single-slot decision: the policy plugin returns { version: vObserved } or
  119        // throws FS_NOT_OBSERVED; the bare default is undefined (unconditional edit).
  120        // No stat — the bare default never manufactures a version basis. The intent
  121        // slot itself can throw FS_NOT_OBSERVED for an unread target, so it sits
  122        // inside the try: both that refusal and the provider's guarded-mutation
  123        // failure get the model-facing remedy below.
  124        let outcome
  125        try {
  126          const intent = await ctx.waterfall('fs/edit-intent', target, exec, () => undefined)
  127          outcome = await ctx.fs.editText(
  128            target,
  129            { oldString: input.oldString, newString: input.newString, replaceAll: input.replaceAll },
  130            intent,
  131            exec.signal,
  132            sandboxPolicy,
  133          )
  134        } catch (error: unknown) {
  135          // A sandbox denial becomes the shared [sandbox: …] marker (the model
  136          // recognizes it from bash); stale/not-observed failures gain their
  137          // model-facing remedy; anything else passes through.
  138          throw remediateFsError(sandbox.mapError(error, sandboxPolicy))
  139        }
  140        // Record the present observation (a no-op when no policy plugin listens).
  141        ctx.emit('fs/observed', target, { kind: 'present', version: outcome.version }, exec)
  142        return {
  143          path: target.displayPath,

  346        if (run.noMatches) return { root, paths: [] }
  347
  348        const all: string[] = []
  349        for (const line of run.stdout.split('\n')) {
  350          if (line.length === 0) continue
  351          const displayPath = toWorkdirRelative(line, run.workdir)
  352          all.push(displayPath)
  353        }
  354        return { root, paths: all }
  355      },

  365      const paths = value.paths

   67  function registration(
   68    id: string,
   69    description: string,
   70    method: string,
   71    query: (input: JsonValue | undefined) => JsonValue | Promise<JsonValue>,
   72    inputSchema: JsonValue = EMPTY_INPUT,
   73    outputSchema: JsonValue = ANY_OUTPUT,
   74  ): HostCordisInspectProviderRegistration {
   75    return {
   76      manifest: {
   77        id,

   89      },

   95  }

   97  function readExact(input: JsonValue | undefined, field: string): string | undefined {
   98    if (input === undefined || input === null || Array.isArray(input) || typeof input !== 'object') return undefined
   99    const value = input[field]
  100    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined

model-callable built-in tool · tool registry · packages/workflow/tool-ralph/src/index.ts:81
Source ↗

Iterate toward one objective with a fresh child and bounded structured handoff per round.

ralph-loop→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
   76    objective: string
   77    maxRounds?: number
   78  }
   79
   80  const RALPH_META = {
   81    name: 'ralph-loop',
   82    description: 'Iterate toward one objective with a fresh child and bounded structured handoff per round.',
   83    phases: [{ title: 'Fresh-agent rounds', detail: 'One clean child context per Ralph round.' }],
   84  }
   85
   86  /**
   87   * Fixed, deployment-owned orchestration. The model supplies data only; it
   88   * cannot alter the loop, provider route, schema, or handoff validation.
   89   */
   90  const RALPH_SCRIPT = String.raw`
   91  const reportSchema = {
   92    type: 'object',
   93    properties: {
   94      status: { type: 'string', enum: ['continue', 'complete', 'blocked'] },
   95      summary: { type: 'string' },
   96      evidence: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
   97      nextSteps: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
   98      blocker: { type: 'string' },
   99    },
  100    required: ['status', 'summary', 'evidence', 'nextSteps', 'blocker'],
  101    additionalProperties: false,
  102  }
  103
  104  function normalizedText(value) {
  105    return typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 && value === value.trim()
  106  }
  107
  108  function normalizedList(value) {
  109    return Array.isArray(value) && value.every(normalizedText)
  110  }
  111
  112  function validateReport(report) {
  113    if (report === null || typeof report !== 'object' || Array.isArray(report)) {
  114      throw new Error('Ralph child returned no structured round report')
  115    }
  116    if (!normalizedText(report.summary)) {
  117      throw new Error('Ralph round report summary must be non-empty and normalized')
  118    }
  119    if (!normalizedList(report.evidence) || !normalizedList(report.nextSteps)) {
  120      throw new Error('Ralph round report evidence and nextSteps must contain only non-empty normalized strings')
  121    }
  122    if (typeof report.blocker !== 'string' || report.blocker !== report.blocker.trim()) {
  123      throw new Error('Ralph round report blocker must be a normalized string')
  124    }
  125    switch (report.status) {
  126      case 'continue':
  127        if (report.nextSteps.length === 0 || report.blocker !== '') {
  128          throw new Error('a continuing Ralph report needs nextSteps and an empty blocker')
  129        }
  130        break
  131      case 'complete':
  132        if (report.evidence.length === 0 || report.nextSteps.length !== 0 || report.blocker !== '') {
  133          throw new Error('a complete Ralph report needs evidence, no nextSteps, and an empty blocker')
  134        }
  135        break
  136      case 'blocked':
  137        if (!normalizedText(report.blocker)) {
  138          throw new Error('a blocked Ralph report needs a concrete blocker')
  139        }
  140        break

  464      active = false

  422        parameters: {
  423          id: { type: 'string', required: true, description: 'Exact session-local schedule id.' },
  424        },
  425        output: { schema: DELETE_OUTPUT_SCHEMA, render: renderValue },
  426        async execute(args, exec): Promise<ScheduleDeleteValue> {
  427          if (args.id.length === 0 || args.id.trim() !== args.id) {
  428            return { code: 'invalid_rule', message: 'schedule_delete id must be non-empty without surrounding whitespace.' }
  429          }
  430          const id = ScheduleId(args.id)
  431          if (exec.agent !== agent) return internalError()
  432          return runCancellableScheduleTransaction(agent, exec.signal, async () => {
  433            const uncertain = await preflight(rootCtx, agent, 'delete', id)
  434            if (uncertain !== undefined) return uncertain
  435            notifyDurableChange()
  436            const folded = foldForTool(agent)
  437            if (isToolError(folded)) return folded
  438            if (!folded.active.some(record => record.id === id)) {
  439              return { id, deleted: false, code: 'schedule_not_found' }
  440            }
  441            const cancelledBeforeAppend = cancellationPlaceholder(exec.signal)
  442            if (cancelledBeforeAppend !== undefined) return cancelledBeforeAppend
  443            try {
  444              agent.session.append('schedule/change', { version: 1, operation: 'delete', id })
  445            } catch {
  446              return internalError()
  447            }
  448            const barrier = await preflight(rootCtx, agent, 'delete', id)
  449            if (barrier !== undefined) return barrier
  450            notifyDurableChange()
  451            return { id, deleted: true }
  452          })
  453        },
  454        presentCall: args => present('Delete reminder', 'other', args.id),
  455      })))
  456    } catch (error) {
  457      for (const dispose of disposers.reverse()) dispose()
  458      throw error
  459    }

   28      description:
   29        'Send a message to a background subagent by its subagent id, continuing the same conversation. It '
   30        + 'becomes the subagent\'s next turn: if it is still working, the message waits until its current turn '
   31        + 'finishes, so it cannot redirect work already underway. This call returns no answer from the '

   44        },

   47        schema: {

   49          additionalProperties: false,
   50          properties: {

   52          },
   53        },
   54        render: (args, _value) => [{
   55          type: 'text',
   56          text: `message queued as the next turn for subagent ${args.subagent_id}`,
   57        }],
   58      },
   59      async execute(args, exec) {
   60        const parent = exec.agent
   61        if (!parent) {
   62          // Parent authority requires an exact live calling agent.
   63          throw new Error('send_message requires a calling agent (exec.agent was undefined)')
   64        }
   65        const message: ContentBlock[] = [{ type: 'text', text: args.message }]
   66        const messageId = await ctx.subagents.followup(
   67          parent,
   68          SessionId(args.subagent_id),
   69          message,
   70          {
   71            source: { kind: 'coordinator', form: 'relay', senderSessionId: parent.id },
   72            signal: exec.signal,
   73          },
   74        )
   75        return { messageId }
   76      },
   77    }))

   79    ctx.tools.register(defineTool({
   80      name: 'interrupt_agent',
   81      description:

   83        + 'direct child or a deeper agent created under you. Only the current turn stops: messages already '
   84        + 'queued for the agent stay parked until a later send_message, agents it started keep running, and '
   85        + 'the agent itself stays available for follow-ups. This call returns as soon as the stop request is '
   86        + 'accepted, so the target may keep running briefly; interrupting an agent that already finished is '

   99                  properties: {
  100                    kind: { type: 'string', required: true, const: 'directory' },
  101                    path: { type: 'string', required: true },
  102                  },
  103                },
  104                {
  105                  type: 'object',
  106                  additionalProperties: false,
  107                  properties: {
  108                    kind: { type: 'string', required: true, const: 'url' },
  109                    url: { type: 'string', required: true },
  110                  },
  111                },
  112                {
  113                  type: 'object',
  114                  additionalProperties: false,
  115                  properties: {
  116                    kind: { type: 'string', required: true, const: 'opaque' },
  117                    description: { type: 'string', required: true },

  121            },

  129          throw new Error(`invalid skill name "${args.name}"`)
  130        }
  131        // The agent is its own scope key, so the lookup resolves the layered
  132        // registry exactly as this agent's composition sees it.
  133        const lookup = { cwd: exec.agent?.session.header.cwd, signal: exec.signal, scope: exec.agent }
  134        const summary = (await ctx.skills.list(lookup)).find(skill => skill.name === args.name)
  135        if (!summary) {
  136          throw new Error(`skill "${args.name}" is unknown or no longer available`)
  137        }
  138        if (!isModelInvocable(summary)) {
  139          throw new Error(`skill "${args.name}" is not available for model invocation`)
  140        }
  141        const skill = await ctx.skills.get(args.name, lookup)

model-callable built-in tool · tool registry · packages/fs/tool-str-replace-editor/src/index.ts:423

Register the model-facing `str_replace_editor` tool. */

  418
  419  /** Register the model-facing `str_replace_editor` tool. */
  420  function registerStrReplaceEditor(ctx: Context, config: ResolvedConfig): void {
  421    const policy = new MutationPolicy(ctx)
  422    ctx.tools.register(defineTool({
  423      name: 'str_replace_editor',
  424      description: config.description,
  425      parameters: {
  426        command: {
  427          type: 'string',
  428          required: true,
  429          enum: ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert'],
  430          description: 'The commands to run. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`.',
  431        },
  432        path: {
  433          type: 'string',
  434          required: true,
  435          description: 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.',
  436        },
  437        file_text: {
  438          type: 'string',
  439          description: 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.',
  440        },
  441        insert_line: {
  442          type: 'integer',
  443          description: 'Required parameter of `insert` command. The `new_str` will be inserted AFTER the line `insert_line` of `path`.',
  444        },
  445        new_str: {
  446          type: 'string',
  447          description: 'Optional parameter of `str_replace` command containing the new string (if not given, no string will be added). Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.',
  448        },
  449        old_str: {
  450          type: 'string',
  451          description: 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.',
  452        },
  453        view_range: {
  454          type: 'array',
  455          items: { type: 'integer' },
  456          description: 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.',
  457        },
  458      },
  459      output: {
  460        schema: { type: 'string' },
  461        render: (_args, value) => [{ type: 'text', text: value }],
  462      },
  463      async execute(args, exec) {
  464        switch (args.command) {
  465          case 'view':
  466            return viewPath(ctx, args.path, args.view_range, config.maxOutputChars, exec)
  467          case 'create':
  468            return createFile(ctx, policy, args.path, args.file_text, exec)
  469          case 'str_replace':

update_goal

model-callable built-in tool · tool registry · packages/goal/tool-goal/src/index.ts:235

  233
  234    ctx.tools.register(defineTool({
  235      name: 'update_goal',
  236      description: 'Update the exact current goal revision. edit, pause, and resume require a direct '
  237        + 'top-level human request. During an automatic continuation of the current goal, complete '
  238        + 'and blocked are also allowed. blocked is rejected before the configured minimum round count; the model remains '
  239        + 'responsible for judging that the same condition persisted across those rounds and must explain it in blocked_reason.',
  240      parameters: {
  241        goal_id: { type: 'string', required: true, description: 'Exact id returned by get_goal.' },
  242        revision: { type: 'number', required: true, description: 'Exact positive revision returned by get_goal.' },
  243        action: {
  244          type: 'string',
  245          required: true,
  246          enum: UPDATE_ACTIONS,
  247          description: 'edit | pause | resume | complete | blocked',
  248        },
  249        objective: { type: 'string', description: 'Replacement objective; valid only with action edit.' },
  250        max_goal_rounds: { type: 'number', description: 'Replacement cap; valid only with action edit.' },
  251        blocked_reason: {
  252          type: 'string',
  253          description: 'Concrete blocking condition; required only with action blocked.',
  254        },
  255      },
  256      output: GOAL_OUTPUT,
  257      execute(args, exec) {
  258        const execution = goalToolExecution(ctx, exec)
  259        const ref = goalRef(args.goal_id, args.revision)
  260        const replacements = {
  261          ...hasText(args.objective) ? { objective: args.objective } : {},
  262          ...hasRoundCap(args.max_goal_rounds) ? { maxGoalRounds: args.max_goal_rounds } : {},
  263        }
  264        if (args.action === 'edit') {
  265          requireDirectHuman(ctx, execution)
  266          if (hasText(args.blocked_reason)) {
  267            throw new HarnessError('blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked', 'GOAL_TOOL_INVALID_UPDATE')
  268          }
  269          const goal = ctx.goals.edit(execution.agent, ref, replacements)
  270          return Promise.resolve(goalValue(goal))
  271        }
  272        if (args.action === 'pause' || args.action === 'resume') {
  273          requireDirectHuman(ctx, execution)
  274          if (hasText(args.objective) || hasRoundCap(args.max_goal_rounds) || hasText(args.blocked_reason)) {
  275            throw new HarnessError(
  276              'objective and max_goal_rounds are valid only with action edit; blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked',
  277              'GOAL_TOOL_INVALID_UPDATE',
  278            )
  279          }
  280          const goal = args.action === 'pause'
  281            ? ctx.goals.pause(execution.agent, ref)
  282            : ctx.goals.resume(execution.agent, ref)
  283          return Promise.resolve(goalValue(goal))
  284        }
  285        const authority = completionAuthority(ctx, execution)
  286        if (hasText(args.objective) || hasRoundCap(args.max_goal_rounds)) {
  287          throw new HarnessError(
  288            'objective and max_goal_rounds are valid only with action edit',
  289            'GOAL_TOOL_INVALID_UPDATE',
  290          )
  291        }
  292        if (args.action === 'complete' && hasText(args.blocked_reason)) {
  293          throw new HarnessError('blocked_reason is valid only with action blocked', 'GOAL_TOOL_INVALID_UPDATE')
  294        }
Open this command's dynamic execution map →

  102      async execute(args: WriteToolArgs, exec) {
  103        const input = parseWriteArgs(args)
  104        // Resolve the per-call sandbox policy (approved mode > session override
  105        // > backend default, plus the session cwd root) BEFORE anything executes;
  106        // an escalating call throws its distinct text on any non-grant.
  107        const sandboxPolicy = await sandbox.resolvePolicy('write', args, exec)
  108        const target = await ctx.fs.resolve(input.filePath, sessionResolveOptions(exec, input.filePath, sandboxPolicy?.workspaceRoot))
  109        // Single-slot decision: the policy plugin produces createIfAbsent/
  110        // replaceIfVersion; the bare default is undefined (unconditional). No stat.
  111        const intent = await ctx.waterfall('fs/write-intent', target, exec, () => undefined)
  112        let outcome: FsWriteOutcome
  113        try {
  114          outcome = await ctx.fs.writeText(target, input.content, intent, exec.signal, sandboxPolicy)
  115        } catch (error: unknown) {
  116          // A sandbox denial becomes the shared [sandbox: …] marker (the model
  117          // recognizes it from bash); stale/not-observed failures gain their
  118          // model-facing remedy; anything else passes through.
  119          throw remediateFsError(sandbox.mapError(error, sandboxPolicy))
  120        }
  121        // Record the present observation (a no-op when no policy plugin listens).
  122        ctx.emit('fs/observed', target, { kind: 'present', version: outcome.version }, exec)
  123        return {
  124          path: target.displayPath,
  125          operation: outcome.operation,
  126          before: outcome.before,
  127          after: outcome.after,
  128        }

   33  function parseGoalCommand(rawInput: string): GoalCommand {
   34    const input = rawInput.trim()
   35    if (input.length === 0) return { kind: 'show' }
   36    const control = input.toLowerCase()
   37    if (control === 'clear') return { kind: 'clear' }
   38    if (control === 'pause') return { kind: 'pause' }
   39    if (control === 'resume') return { kind: 'resume' }
   40    if (control === 'edit') return { kind: 'invalid-edit' }
   41    if (/^edit(?=\s)/iu.test(input)) return { kind: 'edit', objective: input.slice(4).trim() }
   42    return { kind: 'create', objective: input }
   43  }

   78    /* v8 ignore next -- durable replay guarantees every blocked goal carries its validated reason */
   79    if (goal.phase === 'blocked' && reason === undefined) throw new TypeError('blocked goal is missing its reason')
   80    const blocker = reason === undefined ? [] : [`Blocker: ${reason.code}: ${reason.message}`]
   81    return {
   82      kind: 'success',
   83      text: [
   84        title,
   85        `Status: ${phaseLabel(goal.phase)}`,
   86        ...blocker,
   87        `Objective: ${goal.objective}`,
   88        `Rounds: ${goal.roundsStarted}/${goal.maxGoalRounds}`,
   89        `Activation: ${goal.activation}`,
   90        '',
   91        `Commands: ${commandHint(goal)}`,
   92      ].join('\n'),
   93    }
   94  }
   95
   96  /** Exact current compare-and-set ref. */
   97  function goalRef(goal: GoalView): GoalRef {
   98    return { id: goal.id, revision: goal.revision }
   99  }
  100
  101  /** Direct error for an operation that requires a current goal. */
  102  function missingGoal(action: string): CommandResult {
  103    return {
  104      kind: 'error',
  105      text: `No goal is currently set; /goal ${action} requires one. ${USAGE}`,
  106    }
  107  }

   46  function phaseLabel(phase: GoalPhase): string {
   47    switch (phase) {
   48      case 'active': return 'active'
   49      case 'paused': return 'paused'
   50      case 'blocked': return 'blocked'
   51      case 'complete': return 'complete'

   57  /** Commands that are meaningful from one exact live state. */
   58  function commandHint(goal: GoalView): string {
   59    if (goal.phase === 'active') {
   60      return goal.activation === 'armed'
   61        ? '/goal edit <objective>, /goal pause, /goal clear'
   62        : '/goal edit <objective>, /goal resume, /goal clear'
   63    }

   65      case 'paused':
   66      case 'blocked':
   67        return '/goal edit <objective>, /goal resume, /goal clear'
   68      case 'complete':
   69        return '/goal <objective>, /goal clear'
   70      /* v8 ignore next 2 -- the active branch and every non-active phase are handled above */
   71      default: return assertNever(goal.phase, 'goal phase')
   72    }
   73  }

   79    if (goal.phase === 'blocked' && reason === undefined) throw new TypeError('blocked goal is missing its reason')
   80    const blocker = reason === undefined ? [] : [`Blocker: ${reason.code}: ${reason.message}`]

/busy

interactive slash command · dispatch switch · packages/compaction/command-compact/src/index.ts:25
Source ↗

Convert expected capability failures into concise human-only outcomes. */

/busy→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
   20  /* v8 ignore stop */
   21
   22  /** Convert expected capability failures into concise human-only outcomes. */
   23  function expectedFailure(error: ManualCompactionError): CommandResult {
   24    switch (error.code) {
   25      case 'busy':
   26        return {
   27          kind: 'error',
   28          text: 'Compaction is unavailable because this process has an active compaction, or the agent is not idle.',
   29        }
   30      case 'cancelled':
   31        return { kind: 'error', text: 'Compaction cancelled.' }
   32      case 'changed':
   33        return {
   34          kind: 'error',
   35          text: 'The history selected for compaction changed before it could be replaced. The conversation is unchanged; the attempt is recorded in the session log.',
   36        }
   37      case 'summary':
   38        return {
   39          kind: 'error',
   40          text: 'Compaction could not produce a useful summary. The conversation is unchanged; the attempt is recorded in the session log.',
   41        }
   42      case 'commit':
   43        return {
   44          kind: 'error',
   45          text: 'Compaction did not finish cleanly; some session history may have changed. Inspect the current session state before retrying.',
   46        }
   47      case 'persistence':
   48        return {
   49          kind: 'error',
   50          text: 'Compaction finished, but the session could not be saved.',
   51        }
   52      /* v8 ignore next 2 -- ManualCompactionErrorCode is closed and every member is handled above */
   53      default: return assertNever(error.code)
   54    }
   55  }
   56
   57  /** Execute one argument-free manual compaction request. */
   58  async function executeCompact(
   59    ctx: Context,
   60    invocation: CommandInvocation,
   61  ): Promise<CommandResult> {
   62    if (invocation.rawInput.trim().length > 0) {
   63      return { kind: 'error', text: USAGE }
   64    }
   65    try {
   66      const result = await ctx.compaction.compactNow(invocation.agent, invocation.signal, invocation.commandId)
   67      if (result === null) return { kind: 'success', text: 'No compactable history yet.' }
   68      return {
   69        kind: 'success',
   70        text: `Compacted ${result.shadowedSeqs.length} history items (~${result.shadowedTokenCount} tokens).`,
   71        sourceEventSeq: result.summarySeq,
   72      }
   73    } catch (error: unknown) {
   74      if (invocation.signal.aborted) return { kind: 'error', text: 'Compaction cancelled.' }
   75      if (error instanceof ManualCompactionError) return expectedFailure(error)
   76      throw error
   77    }
   78  }
   79
   80  /**
   81   * Register `/compact` for every composed human-command adapter.
   82   * @param ctx - context carrying the command registry and the compaction seam.
   83   */
   84  export function apply(ctx: Context): void {
Open this command's dynamic execution map →

/paused

interactive slash command · dispatch switch · packages/goal/command-goal/src/index.ts:49

Human label for one durable goal phase. */

Expand line-level source evidence
   44
   45  /** Human label for one durable goal phase. */
   46  function phaseLabel(phase: GoalPhase): string {
   47    switch (phase) {
   48      case 'active': return 'active'
   49      case 'paused': return 'paused'
   50      case 'blocked': return 'blocked'
   51      case 'complete': return 'complete'
   52      /* v8 ignore next 2 -- GoalPhase is closed and every member is handled above */
   53      default: return assertNever(phase, 'goal phase')
   54    }
   55  }
   56
   57  /** Commands that are meaningful from one exact live state. */
   58  function commandHint(goal: GoalView): string {
   59    if (goal.phase === 'active') {
   60      return goal.activation === 'armed'
   61        ? '/goal edit <objective>, /goal pause, /goal clear'
   62        : '/goal edit <objective>, /goal resume, /goal clear'
   63    }
   64    switch (goal.phase) {
   65      case 'paused':
   66      case 'blocked':
   67        return '/goal edit <objective>, /goal resume, /goal clear'
   68      case 'complete':
   69        return '/goal <objective>, /goal clear'
   70      /* v8 ignore next 2 -- the active branch and every non-active phase are handled above */
   71      default: return assertNever(goal.phase, 'goal phase')
   72    }
   73  }
   74
   75  /** Render direct UI output without exposing compare-and-set internals. */
   76  function renderGoal(title: string, goal: GoalView): CommandResult {
   77    const reason = goal.phase === 'blocked' ? goal.blockedReason : undefined
   78    /* v8 ignore next -- durable replay guarantees every blocked goal carries its validated reason */
   79    if (goal.phase === 'blocked' && reason === undefined) throw new TypeError('blocked goal is missing its reason')
   80    const blocker = reason === undefined ? [] : [`Blocker: ${reason.code}: ${reason.message}`]
   81    return {
   82      kind: 'success',
   83      text: [
   84        title,
   85        `Status: ${phaseLabel(goal.phase)}`,
   86        ...blocker,
   87        `Objective: ${goal.objective}`,
   88        `Rounds: ${goal.roundsStarted}/${goal.maxGoalRounds}`,
   89        `Activation: ${goal.activation}`,
   90        '',
   91        `Commands: ${commandHint(goal)}`,
   92      ].join('\n'),
   93    }
   94  }
   95
   96  /** Exact current compare-and-set ref. */
   97  function goalRef(goal: GoalView): GoalRef {
   98    return { id: goal.id, revision: goal.revision }
   99  }
  100
  101  /** Direct error for an operation that requires a current goal. */
  102  function missingGoal(action: string): CommandResult {
  103    return {
  104      kind: 'error',
  105      text: `No goal is currently set; /goal ${action} requires one. ${USAGE}`,
  106    }
  107  }

/persistence

interactive slash command · dispatch switch · packages/compaction/command-compact/src/index.ts:47

/persistence→parse→route→policy→handler→receipt
Expand line-level source evidence
   42      case 'commit':
   43        return {
   44          kind: 'error',
   45          text: 'Compaction did not finish cleanly; some session history may have changed. Inspect the current session state before retrying.',
   46        }
   47      case 'persistence':
   48        return {
   49          kind: 'error',
   50          text: 'Compaction finished, but the session could not be saved.',
   51        }
   52      /* v8 ignore next 2 -- ManualCompactionErrorCode is closed and every member is handled above */
   53      default: return assertNever(error.code)
   54    }
   55  }
   56
   57  /** Execute one argument-free manual compaction request. */
   58  async function executeCompact(
   59    ctx: Context,
   60    invocation: CommandInvocation,
   61  ): Promise<CommandResult> {
   62    if (invocation.rawInput.trim().length > 0) {
   63      return { kind: 'error', text: USAGE }
   64    }
   65    try {
   66      const result = await ctx.compaction.compactNow(invocation.agent, invocation.signal, invocation.commandId)
   67      if (result === null) return { kind: 'success', text: 'No compactable history yet.' }
   68      return {
   69        kind: 'success',
   70        text: `Compacted ${result.shadowedSeqs.length} history items (~${result.shadowedTokenCount} tokens).`,
   71        sourceEventSeq: result.summarySeq,
   72      }
   73    } catch (error: unknown) {
   74      if (invocation.signal.aborted) return { kind: 'error', text: 'Compaction cancelled.' }
   75      if (error instanceof ManualCompactionError) return expectedFailure(error)
   76      throw error
   77    }
   78  }
   79
   80  /**
   81   * Register `/compact` for every composed human-command adapter.
   82   * @param ctx - context carrying the command registry and the compaction seam.
   83   */
   84  export function apply(ctx: Context): void {
   85    const active = new Set<Promise<CommandResult>>()
   86    const handler = (invocation: CommandInvocation): Promise<CommandResult> => {
   87      const operation = executeCompact(ctx, invocation)
   88      active.add(operation)
   89      const retire = (): void => { active.delete(operation) }
   90      // Both branches retire without rethrowing, so the derived observer promise
   91      // cannot become an unhandled mirror of an expected handler rejection.
   92      void operation.then(retire, retire)
   93      return operation
   94    }
   95
   96    ctx.effect(function* () {
   97      // Yield drain before registration: composite teardown is LIFO, so no new
   98      // invocation can enter while already-started handler promises quiesce.
   99      yield async () => { await Promise.allSettled(active) }
  100      yield ctx.commands.register({
  101        name: 'compact',
  102        description: 'Compact older conversation history',
  103        handler,
  104      })
  105    }, 'command-compact lifecycle')
  106  }
Open this command's dynamic execution map →

  110  function executeGoalCommand(ctx: Context, invocation: CommandInvocation): CommandResult {
  111    const command = parseGoalCommand(invocation.rawInput)
  112    try {
  113      const current = ctx.goals.get(invocation.agent)
  114      switch (command.kind) {
  115        case 'show':
  116          return current === undefined
  117            ? { kind: 'success', text: `No goal is currently set.\n${USAGE}` }
  118            : renderGoal('Goal', current)
  119        case 'invalid-edit':
  120          return { kind: 'error', text: `Goal editing requires a replacement objective.\n${USAGE}` }
  121        case 'create':
  122          if (current !== undefined && current.phase !== 'complete') {
  123            return {
  124              kind: 'error',
  125              text: `A goal is already ${phaseLabel(current.phase)}. Use /goal edit <objective> to change it or /goal clear before replacing it.`,
  126            }
  127          }
  128          return renderGoal('Goal created', ctx.goals.create(invocation.agent, { objective: command.objective }))
  129        case 'edit':
  130          if (current === undefined) return missingGoal('edit')
  131          if (current.phase === 'complete') {
  132            return renderGoal('Goal created', ctx.goals.create(invocation.agent, { objective: command.objective }))
  133          }
  134          return renderGoal(
  135            'Goal updated',
  136            ctx.goals.edit(invocation.agent, goalRef(current), { objective: command.objective }),
  137          )
  138        case 'pause':
  139          if (current === undefined) return missingGoal('pause')
  140          return renderGoal('Goal paused', ctx.goals.pause(invocation.agent, goalRef(current)))
  141        case 'resume':
  142          if (current === undefined) return missingGoal('resume')
  143          return renderGoal('Goal resumed', ctx.goals.resume(invocation.agent, goalRef(current)))
  144        case 'clear':
  145          if (current === undefined) return { kind: 'success', text: 'No goal to clear.' }
  146          ctx.goals.clear(invocation.agent, goalRef(current))
  147          return { kind: 'success', text: 'Goal cleared.' }
  148        /* v8 ignore next 2 -- GoalCommand is closed and every member is handled above */
  149        default: return assertNever(command, 'goal command')
  150      }
  151    } catch (error: unknown) {
  152      if (error instanceof GoalError) {
  153        return {
  154          kind: 'error',
  155          text: 'The goal command is not valid for the current state. Run /goal to view available commands.',
  156        }
  157      }
  158      throw error
  159    }
  160  }
  161
  162  /** Register the Codex-shaped `/goal` command for every composed command adapter. */
  163  export function apply(ctx: Context): void {
  164    ctx.commands.register({
  165      name: 'goal',
  166      description: 'set or view the goal for a long-running task',
  167      input: { hint: '[<objective>|clear|edit <objective>|pause|resume]' },
  168      handler: invocation => executeGoalCommand(ctx, invocation),
  169    })
  170  }

   56
   57  /** Execute one argument-free manual compaction request. */
   58  async function executeCompact(
   59    ctx: Context,
   60    invocation: CommandInvocation,
   61  ): Promise<CommandResult> {
   62    if (invocation.rawInput.trim().length > 0) {
   63      return { kind: 'error', text: USAGE }
   64    }
   65    try {
   66      const result = await ctx.compaction.compactNow(invocation.agent, invocation.signal, invocation.commandId)
   67      if (result === null) return { kind: 'success', text: 'No compactable history yet.' }
   68      return {
   69        kind: 'success',
   70        text: `Compacted ${result.shadowedSeqs.length} history items (~${result.shadowedTokenCount} tokens).`,
   71        sourceEventSeq: result.summarySeq,
   72      }
   73    } catch (error: unknown) {
   74      if (invocation.signal.aborted) return { kind: 'error', text: 'Compaction cancelled.' }
   75      if (error instanceof ManualCompactionError) return expectedFailure(error)
   76      throw error
   77    }
   78  }
   79
   80  /**
   81   * Register `/compact` for every composed human-command adapter.
   82   * @param ctx - context carrying the command registry and the compaction seam.
   83   */
   84  export function apply(ctx: Context): void {
   85    const active = new Set<Promise<CommandResult>>()
   86    const handler = (invocation: CommandInvocation): Promise<CommandResult> => {
   87      const operation = executeCompact(ctx, invocation)
   88      active.add(operation)
   89      const retire = (): void => { active.delete(operation) }
   90      // Both branches retire without rethrowing, so the derived observer promise
   91      // cannot become an unhandled mirror of an expected handler rejection.
   92      void operation.then(retire, retire)
   93      return operation
   94    }

L4factarchitecture

01 · Cordis composes the whole harness as a reconfigurable plugin tree

SOURCE FACT

architecture.md lists model adapters, tools, session logs, sandbox, and the agent loop as Cordis plugins; profiles select bundles and bundles provide layered patches.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

Instead of one giant Agent class full of conditionals, the runtime is a tree whose branches can be replaced.

IMPLICATION

A new harness should expose replaceable service seams and print the resolved boot configuration.

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 8–32
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 33–58
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

docs/architecture.md:8–58PINNED ↗
    8
    9  ## Cordis
   10
   11  [Cordis](cordis-primer.md) is the framework under dsh: plugins contribute services, typed events, and reversible effects to a shared context. Every part of the product is a plugin, including the model adapter, the tool registry, the session log, and the agent loop itself, so every part is replaceable from configuration.
   12
   13  There is no privileged core to patch: you extend dsh by mounting a plugin beside the others, and registrations are effects that unwind when their plugin unloads.
   14
   15  ## Profiles and bundles
   16
   17  A running `dsh` is a plugin tree composed at boot from ordered layers.
   18
   19  A **profile** is a named composition stored in the Harness home. It lists the bundles it stacks, holds any out-of-tree plugins it installs, and keeps the user's own `cordis.patch.yml`. `web` and `headless` ship as templates.
   20
   21  A **bundle** is a distribution format for Cordis config rows and the code they mount, so whatever it inserts stays patchable by the layers above it.
   22
   23  Each declares itself in its own `package.json` under a `dsh` field: `dsh.profile` lists a profile's bundles, and `dsh.bundle` points at a bundle's patch file.
   24
   25  [`dsh-base`](../packages/bundle/base/README.md) is the first layer of every profile: model adapters, tools, persistence, sandbox and approval policy, settings, credentials, telemetry. [`dsh-web-app`](../packages/bundle/web-app/README.md) adds the browser application; [`dsh-headless`](../packages/bundle/headless/README.md) adds a one-shot runner with no server at all.
   26
   27  Layers apply to an empty entry list in this order: each bundle in the profile's listed order, then the profile's `cordis.patch.yml`, then the home-level one, then any `--patch` overlay. A patch targets a row by id and replaces its whole config, or inserts new rows.
   28
   29  To see the tree your machine actually boots:
   30
   31  ```sh
   32  dsh --profile web --dump-config
   33  ```
   34
   35  Any row it prints can be replaced by a patch of your own.
   36
   37  Composition mechanics are in [app-boot](../packages/boot/app-boot/README.md#profiles); config fields are in the generated [config catalog](config-catalog.md).
   38
   39  ## Core packages
   40
   41  Here are some core packages that contribute to the Cordis tree.
   42
   43  | Package | Owns | `ctx` key |
   44  |---|---|---|
   45  | [`core/session`](subsystems/session.md) | The append-only `SessionEvent` log and in-memory store | `ctx.sessions` |
   46  | [`core/system-prompt`](subsystems/system-prompt.md) | Prompt-section and tool-schema assembly | `ctx.systemPrompt` |
   47  | [`core/tools`](subsystems/tools.md) | The scoped tool registry and guarded execution pipeline | `ctx.tools` |
   48  | [`core/agent`](subsystems/core.md) | The `Agent` interface, live registry, and `agent/*` events | `ctx.agents` |
   49  | [`core/agent-loop`](subsystems/core.md) | The default driver implementing that interface | `ctx.agentLoop` |
   50  | [`core/scope`](subsystems/scope.md) | The per-agent scoped-registration primitive | library, no key |
   51  | [`llm/llm`](subsystems/llm-streaming.md) | Message and stream vocabulary plus the adapter seam | `ctx.llm` |
   52
   53  ## Events
   54
   55  Events are the extension points, and picking the right domain is the first decision in most changes.
   56
   57  - **Session events** are durable facts appended to the log and broadcast through `session/event`. Use one when the fact must survive a reload.
   58  - **Agent events** (`agent/*`) carry a live `Agent`: inbox, step, status, request, validation, continuation. Use one to observe or intercept work in flight.
   59  - **Capability events** attach policy and adapters to a seam (`fs/*`, `tools/*`, `telemetry/*`) without importing the loop.
   60
   61  The [event map](event-producer-consumer.md) lists every event's producers and consumers.
   62
   63  ## Turn flow
   64
   65  A **step** is one model request plus the tools it calls. A **turn** is zero or more steps: it opens before its first input is claimed and closes once nothing is owed.
   66
   67  ```text
   68  turn/start
   69    claim next-step input plus one queued message
   70    assemble prompt sections + tool schemas
   71    -> agent/pre-step                   reject | enter(messages)
   72       reject, or a first enter rewritten empty -> close the turn with no step
   73       step/start
   74       append entered messages as user/message
   75       derive model history from the log
   76       agent/request -> llm/stream -> assistant/chunk* -> assistant/message
   77       tool/call* -> tools/pre-execute -> tools/execute -> tools/post-execute -> tool/result*
   78       step/end
   79       tools owe another request, or next-step input arrived -> claim -> next step
   80    -> agent/turn-stopping
   81  turn/end
   82  ```
   83
   84  `turn/*`, `step/*`, `user/message`, `assistant/*`, and `tool/*` are durable session events; the rest are live extension points across three domains. `agent/pre-step`, `agent/request`, `llm/stream`, and the three `tools/*` events are waterfalls, whose listeners must call `next()` to delegate; `agent/turn-stopping` is serial and has no `next()`.
   85
   86  Input reaches the driver through one inbox. Some messages wake it immediately; injected context waits in the inbox until another message does.
   87
   88  `agent/pre-step` decides what the model sees. Listeners may rewrite the claimed messages or reject them outright; a rejected or empty first claim still closes a durable turn that spent no step, so the log records the attempt. Each step reads the prompt sections and tool schemas that plugins registered.
   89
   90  Details: the [sequence diagram](agent-lifecycle.md), the [tool pipeline](tool-execution-pipeline.md), and [cancellation and error recovery](subsystems/core.md#the-agent-handle).
   91
   92  ## Session log
   93
   94  The session log is the source of the context the model sees. `deriveMessages()` projects model history from it, and raw `assistant/chunk` events preserve replay and UI fidelity. Fork, resume, transcripts, telemetry, and persistence all derive from this stream.
   95
   96  **Model-visible means logged.** Anything that reaches a model request must be reconstructable from the log, and a runtime invariant asserts it. This is why a new model-visible input requires a new session event: extend `SessionEventMap` and render from the log.
   97
   98  ## Capability seams
   99
  100  A **seam** is a swappable capability with three roles: a **Service Definition** declaring the interface, a **Service Provider** implementing it, and a **Consumer** using it, commonly a model-facing tool. A package may combine roles, but one role alone is not a seam; adding a capability means designing all three ([capability graph](capability-seams.md)).
  101
  102  Seams are why one provider swap changes the whole product. Filesystem and subprocess providers share one execution world, so pointing them at a remote sandbox moves Bash, PTY, and LSP with them, with no provider forks. [Subagent providers](subsystems/subagent.md) vary just as widely behind one interface, from a fresh child agent to a delegated turn in another product.
  103
  104  ## Where new behavior goes
  105
  106  New behavior attaches to a documented extension point. Changing the loop itself updates this map.
  107
  108  | Goal | Mechanism |
  109  |---|---|
  110  | Add a model provider | register its adapter on `ctx.llm` |
  111  | Add a model-facing capability | register on `ctx.tools`; its schema joins prompt assembly |
  112  | Give one session a different capability set | compose an agent preset; a service row there needs an `isolate` realm |
  113  | Add shell execution | register a `ctx.shell` backend; the local one spawns through `ctx.subprocess` |
  114  | Add persistent terminal execution | register a `ctx.terminals` backend plus `dsh-tool-terminal` |
  115  | Add a human command | register on `ctx.commands`; it dispatches without a model turn |
  116  | Add background work | register on `ctx.jobs`; `job_*` tools collect or stop it |
  117  | Add filesystem access or policy | register a `ctx.fs` provider or listen to `fs/*` events |
  118  | Confine spawned processes | use a `ctx.sandbox` backend; consumers wrap argv before spawning |
  119  | Intercept a request, tool, or turn | use its `agent/*` or `tools/*` event; `agent/turn-stopping` stops a turn |
  120  | Add model-facing context | call `agent.inject()`; it lands in the next admitted request |
  121  | Add UI or editor integration | drive `ctx.agents` and render from `session/event` |
  122  | Add a Web Client Chat node | register a `ConversationNodeDefinition` + keyed renderer |
  123  | Add durable session state | extend `SessionEventMap`; render and replay from the log |
  124  | Generate session titles | register the sole `ctx.sessionTitle` provider |
  125  | Manage a same-session objective | use `ctx.goals`; continue through `agent/*` |
  126  | Fork a live session | `ctx.sessions.fork(source, boundary?, childSessionId?)` |
  127  | Scope a registration to one agent | use that agent's `agent.ctx` |
  128
  129  The [extension cookbook](cookbook/extension-cookbook.md) maps features to capabilities and indexes the step-by-step guides for [packages](cookbook/adding-a-package.md), [tools](cookbook/adding-a-tool.md), [LLM adapters](cookbook/adding-an-llm-adapter.md), and [Chat nodes](cookbook/adding-a-conversation-node.md).

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · Agent, Session, LLM, and tools meet through Context services

SOURCE FACT

The agent-loop constructor creates the event dispatcher, Inbox, Scope, and RuntimeContextProjection, then attaches them to the Cordis context.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

The Agent drives the work; tools, logs, and prompt assembly come from context instead of direct cross-package imports.

IMPLICATION

Design extensions around context services so UI or one provider cannot invade the main loop.

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 63–79
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 80–97
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:63–97PINNED ↗
   63  /** Drives one session through turn and step boundaries. */
   64  export class ReactLoopAgent implements Agent {
   65    readonly inbox: Inbox
   66    private phase: Phase
   67    private activityDone: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve()
   68
   69    /** The agent-scoped registration boundary; the lifecycle owner unwinds it after the driver exits. */
   70    readonly scope: Scope
   71    readonly ctx: Context
   72
   73    /** Fused dispatcher, built once in the constructor so hot-path dispatches never allocate. */
   74    private readonly dispatch: AgentEventDispatch
   75
   76    /** Whether this loop instance has appended its initial/resume request anchor. */
   77    private requestHeaderLogged = false
   78    private readonly runtimeContext: RuntimeContextProjection
   79
   80    constructor(
   81      private loopCtx: Context,
   82      public readonly id: SessionId,
   83      public readonly options: AgentOptions,
   84      public readonly session: Session,
   85    ) {
   86      this.dispatch = agentEvents(loopCtx, this)
   87      this.inbox = new Inbox(session, {
   88        inserted: (message) => { this.dispatch.emit('agent/inbox/inserted', { message }) },
   89        discarded: (message) => { this.dispatch.emit('agent/inbox/discarded', { message }) },
   90        claimed: (message, turn) => { this.dispatch.emit('agent/inbox/claimed', { message, turn }) },
   91      })
   92      const lastTurn = session.events.findLast(event => event.type === 'turn/start')?.data.turn ?? 0
   93      this.phase = { kind: 'idle', lastTurn }
   94      this.scope = createScope(loopCtx, this)
   95      this.ctx = this.scope.ctx.extend({ agent: this })
   96      this.runtimeContext = new RuntimeContextProjection(this.ctx, session)
   97    }
   98
   99    get status(): AgentStatus {
  100      return this.phase.kind === 'idle' || this.phase.kind === 'maintenance' ? 'idle' : 'running'
  101    }
  102
  103    /** Commit a phase and publish its externally visible status transition. */
  104    private setPhase(next: Phase): void {
  105      const previousStatus = this.status
  106      this.phase = next
  107      const status = this.status
  108      if (status !== previousStatus) {
  109        this.dispatch.emit('agent/status', { status })
  110      }
  111    }
  112
  113    send(message: UserMessage, target: InboxTarget, wakeup: boolean): void {
  114      // Waking input cannot join an aborted activity, so it starts the next turn.
  115      // Captured before the insertion so a reentrant cancel from a splice observer cannot reclassify it.
  116      const wakingAfterAbort = wakeup && this.phase.kind !== 'idle' && this.phase.abort.signal.aborted
  117      const resolvedTarget = wakingAfterAbort ? 'next-turn' : target
  118      this.inbox.splice(resolvedTarget, Infinity, 0, [message])
  119      if (wakeup) this.wakeDriver(wakingAfterAbort)
  120    }
  121
  122    followup(input: UserMessage): void {
  123      this.send(input, 'next-turn', true)
  124    }
  125
  126    steer(input: UserMessage): void {
  127      this.send(input, 'next-step', true)
  128    }
  129
  130    inject(input: UserMessage): void {
  131      this.send(input, 'next-step', false)
  132    }
  133
  134    cancel(cause: AgentCancelCause, options: CancelOptions = {}): void {
  135      if (!options.keepInbox) {
  136        this.inbox.clear()
  137        if (this.phase.kind !== 'idle') this.phase.wakeRequested = false
  138      }
  139      if (this.phase.kind !== 'idle') this.phase.abort.abort(cause)
  140    }
  141
  142    runMaintenance<T>(job: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  143      if (this.phase.kind !== 'idle') throw new Error(`agent "${this.id}" already has active work`)
  144      const done = Promise.withResolvers<void>()
  145      const maintenance: Phase = {
  146        kind: 'maintenance',
  147        abort: new AbortController(),
  148        lastTurn: this.phase.lastTurn,
  149        wakeRequested: false,
  150      }
  151      this.setPhase(maintenance)
  152      this.activityDone = done.promise
  153      return (async () => {
  154        try {
  155          return await job(maintenance.abort.signal)
  156        } finally {
  157          this.setPhase({ kind: 'idle', lastTurn: maintenance.lastTurn })
  158          if (maintenance.wakeRequested && this.inbox.hasPending) this.wakeDriver()
  159          done.resolve()
  160        }
  161      })()
  162    }
  163
  164    /**
  165     * Start one driver, or latch its wake behind maintenance or an aborted
  166     * activity. A wake sent while idle always opens its turn boundary, even
  167     * when its message was cleared; only a latched replay is suppressed when
  168     * the queue no longer holds the wake.

02
DIMENSION · LOOP

Control loop

2 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factloop

01 · A turn contains steps, and every boundary is logged

SOURCE FACT

turn() appends turn/start, enters preStep, records step/start, user/message, and step/end, then closes with turn/end.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

A user task is not one request; it is a replayable sequence of work orders.

IMPLICATION

Do not retain only the final answer; state-machine boundaries are recovery and observability data.

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 245–286
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 287–330
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:245–330PINNED ↗
  245    /** Open one turn before claiming its first proposed step. */
  246    private async turn(): Promise<boolean> {
  247      if (this.phase.kind !== 'running') {
  248        this.throwError(new Error(`agent "${this.id}": turn without driver reservation`))
  249      }
  250      const phase = this.phase
  251      const { signal } = phase.abort
  252      signal.throwIfAborted()
  253      const turn = phase.turn + 1
  254      try {
  255        this.session.append('turn/start', { turn })
  256      } catch (error: unknown) {
  257        this.throwError(error)
  258      }
  259      phase.turn = turn
  260      let turnEnds: TurnEndReason | null = null
  261      let target: InboxTarget = 'next-turn'
  262      try {
  263        while (true) {
  264          signal.throwIfAborted()
  265          const step = phase.step + 1
  266          const decision = await this.preStep(target, { turn, step })
  267          if (decision.kind === 'reject') {
  268            turnEnds = { kind: 'blocked' }
  269            return false
  270          }
  271          if (turnEnds && decision.messages.length === 0) break
  272          // A removed waking message or an enter decision rewritten to empty
  273          // still owns the initial turn boundary, but it spends no model call.
  274          if (phase.step === 0 && decision.messages.length === 0) {
  275            turnEnds = { kind: 'completed' }
  276            return false
  277          }
  278          signal.throwIfAborted()
  279          this.session.append('step/start', { turn, step })
  280          phase.step = step
  281          try {
  282            for (const message of decision.messages) {
  283              this.session.append('user/message', message, { surfaceOp: 'append' })
  284            }
  285            // max-tokens is sticky: once any step hits the ceiling, later steps
  286            // that complete normally must not downgrade the turn outcome.
  287            const stepEnd = await this.step(decision.assembly)
  288            // max-tokens stays sticky: a later completed step must not
  289            // downgrade the turn outcome.
  290            if (turnEnds === null || turnEnds.kind !== 'max-tokens') turnEnds = stepEnd
  291          } finally {
  292            this.session.append('step/end', { turn, step })
  293          }
  294          signal.throwIfAborted()
  295          if (turnEnds && this.inbox.nextStep.length === 0) {
  296            await this.dispatch.serial('agent/turn-stopping', { turn, signal })
  297            signal.throwIfAborted()
  298          }
  299          if (turnEnds && this.inbox.nextStep.length === 0) break
  300          target = 'next-step'
  301        }
  302      } catch (error: unknown) {
  303        if (signal.aborted) {
  304          turnEnds = { kind: 'aborted', reason: signal.reason as AgentCancelCause }
  305          throw error
  306        }
  307        // Every failure is structured: an `LlmError` keeps its facts, anything
  308        // else flattens to `errorChain` text under the `UNKNOWN` code.
  309        turnEnds = {
  310          kind: 'error',
  311          error: error instanceof LlmError
  312            ? error.failure
  313            : { message: errorChain(error), code: 'UNKNOWN' },
  314        }
  315        this.throwError(error)
  316      } finally {
  317        try {
  318          // oxlint-disable-next-line typescript/no-non-null-assertion -- every exit assigns a turn ending
  319          this.session.append('turn/end', { turn, reason: turnEnds! })
  320        } catch (error: unknown) {
  321          this.throwError(error)
  322        }
  323      }
  324      if (!this.inbox.hasPending) return false
  325      phase.abort = new AbortController()
  326      // A fresh controller makes a latch set on the old one stale: the live driver claims the queue itself.
  327      phase.wakeRequested = false
  328      phase.step = 0
  329      return true
  330    }
  331
  332    private async step(assembly: PromptAssembly): Promise<StepEndReason | null> {
  333      /* v8 ignore next -- private callers establish the running phase before executing a step */
  334      if (this.phase.kind !== 'running') throw new Error(`agent "${this.id}": step outside running phase`)
  335      const { turn, step, abort: { signal } } = this.phase
  336      signal.throwIfAborted()
  337      const system = renderPrompt(assembly)
  338
  339      while (true) {
  340        const { request, preparedCall } = await this.buildRequest(
  341          turn, step, assembly.tools, system, this.session.deriveMessages(), signal,
  342        )
  343        const assembler = new BlockAssembler()
  344        const chunkSeqs: number[] = []
  345        const stream = preparedCall?.stream(request) ?? this.loopCtx.llm.stream(request)
  346        signal.throwIfAborted()
  347        for await (const chunk of stream) {
  348          signal.throwIfAborted()
  349          chunkSeqs.push(this.session.append('assistant/chunk', { turn, step, chunk }).seq)
  350          assembler.push(chunk)
  351        }
  352        signal.throwIfAborted()
  353        const finish = assembler.finish
  354        if (finish.kind === 'error' || finish.kind === 'aborted') {
  355          const action = await this.dispatch.waterfall(
  356            'agent/request-error', {
  357              turn,
  358              step,
  359              provider: request.provider,
  360              failure: finish.failure,
  361              retryPolicy: preparedCall?.retryPolicy,
  362              signal,
  363            },
  364            () => Promise.resolve<RequestErrorAction>(undefined),
  365          )
  366          signal.throwIfAborted()
  367          if (action?.kind !== 'retry') {
  368            throw new LlmError(finish.failure.message, finish.failure.code, finish.failure)
  369          }
  370          continue
  371        }
  372
  373        const message = createAssistantMessage({
  374          content: assembler.blocks(),
  375          source: {
  376            provider: request.provider,
  377            model: request.model,
  378            ...assembler.replayState !== undefined ? { replayState: assembler.replayState } : {},
  379          },
  380        })
  381        this.session.append(
  382          'assistant/message',
  383          {
  384            turn,
  385            step,
  386            message,
  387            ...assembler.usage === undefined ? {} : { usage: assembler.usage },
  388          },
  389          { surfaceOp: 'append', sourceEventSeqs: chunkSeqs },
  390        )
  391        if (finish.kind === 'max-tokens') return { kind: 'max-tokens' }
  392
  393        const toolCalls = message.content.filter(block => block.type === 'tool-call')
  394        if (toolCalls.length === 0) return { kind: 'completed' }
  395        const { concluded } = await executeToolCalls(
  396          this.loopCtx, turn, step, toolCalls, signal,
  397          context => this.inbox.splice('next-step', this.inbox.nextStep.length, 0, [context]),
  398        )
  399        return concluded ? { kind: 'completed' } : null
  400      }
  401    }

02
L1factloop

02 · pre-step is an interceptable gate before a model request

SOURCE FACT

preStep claims inbox input, assembles prompt and runtime context, then runs the agent/pre-step waterfall where listeners may rewrite messages or reject.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

Before the model sees a request, plugins may add context or explicitly reject the step.

IMPLICATION

Approval, planning, budgets, and dynamic context belong at an explicit pre-request seam.

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 225–233
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 234–243
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

packages/core/agent-loop/src/agent.ts:225–243PINNED ↗
  225    private async preStep(target: InboxTarget, position: { turn: number; step: number }): Promise<PreparedStep> {
  226      /* v8 ignore next -- private callers establish the running phase before proposing a step */
  227      if (this.phase.kind !== 'running') throw new Error(`agent "${this.id}": pre-step outside running phase`)
  228      const signal = this.phase.abort.signal
  229      const claimed = this.inbox.claim(target, position.turn)
  230      const assembly = await this.loopCtx.systemPrompt.assemble(assembleContextFor(this, signal))
  231      signal.throwIfAborted()
  232      const sections = renderContextSections(assembly)
  233      const context = this.runtimeContext.project(joinContextSections(sections), sections)
  234      const decision = await this.dispatch.waterfall(
  235        'agent/pre-step', { messages: claimed, ...position, signal },
  236        (): Promise<PreStepDecision> => Promise.resolve<PreStepDecision>({
  237          kind: 'enter',
  238          messages: context === undefined ? claimed : [...claimed, context],
  239        }),
  240      )
  241      signal.throwIfAborted()
  242      return decision.kind === 'reject' ? decision : { ...decision, assembly }
  243    }
  244

DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

Model & context

4 located findings; each moves from fact to plain language, engineering impact, and source excerpt.

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · Stream chunks enter the log before BlockAssembler creates a message

SOURCE FACT

step() calls llm.stream, appends every assistant/chunk, feeds BlockAssembler, then records assistant/message with sourceEventSeqs.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

The UI can show partial text while recovery still knows which chunks formed the message.

IMPLICATION

Streaming UI, audit replay, and model messages should share one causal record.

LINE-BY-LINE READING
  1. 332–360
    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 361–391
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.

  403    /**
  404     * Compose one frozen request and bind it to the adapter registration that
  405     * resolved its exact-model defaults.
  406     */
  407    private async buildRequest(
  408      turn: number,
  409      step: number,
  410      tools: GenerateOptions['tools'] & object,
  411      system: string,
  412      boundaryMessages: Message[],
  413      signal: AbortSignal,
  414    ): Promise<{ request: GenerateOptions; preparedCall?: PreparedLlmCall }> {
  415      const { session } = this
  416
  417      // A loop instance starts from its declared route, restoring only an explicit
  418      // effort owned by that exact model. Later steps re-resolve marked defaults.
  419      const persistedHeader = session.requestHeader()
  420      const persistedConfig = persistedHeader?.config
  421      const route = { provider: this.options.provider ?? '', model: this.options.model ?? '' }
  422      const reasoningEffort = persistedConfig?.provider === route.provider
  423        && persistedConfig.model === route.model
  424        && persistedHeader?.adapterDefaults?.reasoningEffort !== true
  425        ? persistedConfig.reasoningEffort
  426        : undefined
  427      const maxTokens = this.options.maxTokens
  428      const seedConfig = deepFreeze(structuredClone(
  429        this.requestHeaderLogged
  430          // oxlint-disable-next-line typescript/no-non-null-assertion -- the instance logged the header it now folds
  431          ? requestProposal(persistedHeader!)
  432          : {
  433            ...route,
  434            ...reasoningEffort === undefined ? {} : { reasoningEffort },
  435            ...maxTokens === undefined ? {} : { maxTokens },
  436          },
  437      ))
  438      const proposedConfig = await this.dispatch.waterfall(
  439        'agent/request', { turn, step, signal },
  440        () => Promise.resolve(seedConfig),
  441      )
  442      signal.throwIfAborted()
  443      if (!proposedConfig.provider || !proposedConfig.model) {
  444        throw new Error(`agent "${this.id}" has no provider/model: set AgentOptions.provider and AgentOptions.model or supply both via the agent/request waterfall`)
  445      }
  446      let config: LlmCallConfig
  447      let preparedCall: PreparedLlmCall | undefined
  448      try {
  449        preparedCall = await this.loopCtx.llm.prepareCall(proposedConfig, signal)
  450        config = preparedCall.config
  451      } catch (error: unknown) {
  452        // Middleware may serve an unregistered route; terminal dispatch still requires an adapter.
  453        if (!(error instanceof LlmError) || error.code !== 'NO_ADAPTER') throw error
  454        config = proposedConfig
  455      }
  456      signal.throwIfAborted()
  457
  458      const header = canonicalHeader({
  459        config,
  460        ...preparedCall === undefined ? {} : { adapterDefaults: preparedCall.adapterDefaults },
  461        ...system ? { system } : {},
  462        ...tools.length > 0 ? { tools } : {},

L2factmodelContext

02 · Provider failures carry structured code, status, and request facts

SOURCE FACT

LlmFailure defines message, code, status, retry delay, and request id; request-error decides retry while other failures remain LlmError.

PLAIN LANGUAGE

A failure is not just a string; the harness can distinguish auth, rate limits, missing adapters, and transport faults.

IMPLICATION

Error taxonomy must feed replay and retry policy instead of being guessed by the UI.

LINE-BY-LINE READING

    Inputs, configuration, and state

    Identify who owns the input, which configuration is read, and which state is created.

  2. 45–51
    Branches, effects, and receipts

    Trace conditions, external calls, event writes, and failure exits instead of reading only names.

  3. after
    Return to the caller

    Follow the caller and the next event to see how this code's result is consumed.
